The issue was incomplete provider/protocol adaptation, not merely model picker presentation. The previous desktop greeting and user-authorized project fork remain retained.

Updated integrations/codex_antigravity/gateway.py, worker.py, management formatting, README, and docs/operations/codex-antigravity-compatibility.md. Gateway now preserves custom grammar, supports forced custom selection, accepts exact unambiguous advertised canonical tool names, unwraps known input envelopes or preserves native freeform input, and forwards function arguments unchanged for native Codex validation/error recovery. It never executes those tools remotely. Added JSON-line worker EOF control to close the isolated provider client, actual resolved model and provider/startup timings, per-request diagnostic records, and sanitized unknown-name diagnostics. Client permissions and grammar validation remain enforced. No OpenAI tokens are sent over SSH, no prompt/tool argument/output logs added, and no shared Hermes/specialist/database service restarted.

Current isolated gateway PID 62896, healthy and idle after verification. Current worker hash is in settings.json and freeform-fix-release.json; final-release-verification.json confirms worker/settings and running gateway/source hash agreement. User default model was observed as antigravity/gemini-3.8-flash during final verification, a later desktop preference relative to earlier notes; it was preserved, not overwritten back to GPT. Installed merged catalog remains standard; reduced-tool candidate is not installed.

Meaningful checks: focused Ruff check passes, Python sources parse, ten adapter/isolated stream-lifecycle contract checks pass with zero inference. Fake-provider EOF probe proves close/exit plumbing only, not desktop Stop or production account lease release. Actual corrected installed-catalog native Codex check passed (native-standard-v2-result.json): shell read input, apply_patch, real local read-only MCP nonce, shell read output, exact nonces in final response, independent exact file-content assertion, CLI exit 0. Token report: cumulative 220871 input, 576 output. Earlier JSONDecodeError native-standard-final failure and original standalone outer-reader ACL failure are retained. No security/ACL weakening. Isolated candidate code_mode_only first failed unknown tool; corrected candidate passed exact shell/patch/MCP/output assertions, but used ten model requests and 437273 input/2494 output tokens with repeated unchanged-file reads, so no performance-mode switch was made. Stop probe found that candidate already exited and stopped no process.

Limits remain explicit: this is Gemini Flash bounded tool-loop evidence, not full equivalence, every model/effort or all external services. Images/audio/hosted web_search/server continuation/remote compaction are unsupported; long-context automatic recovery and desktop stop behavior unverified; no custom desktop performance panel was implemented. Only the fixture MCP had ephemeral per-tool approval; no global permissions or real external writes. Existing provider-aware Trade-Enabled NanoCentralised OGPV fork retains obsolete saved C: cwd: user must choose existing Z:/Hardware-Lab-Workspace through desktop UI before project work; do not recreate removed workspaces or rewrite session DBs. Standalone Restore Codex OpenAI shortcut remains recovery without Codex/Python/gateway; preview previously passed, actual restore not executed while integration is in use. No automatic desktop UI operation was attempted.

Full capability matrix and evidence scopes are in docs/operations/codex-antigravity-compatibility.md; private receipts are under .uuma-local/codex-antigravity-ui-20261102. Preserve all unrelated Scholar v665 work and pending nightly/release/deep-job constraints in the entire prior shared handoff below. Mission remains verification/pending_review; no gate approval, commit or broad deployment.

# Progress v663: pinned, guarded night dimension admissions

On 2026-11-03 Malaysia, read-only metadata preflight reconfirmed the same four held initial-reading dimension failures. `pin_dimension_cases_v663.py` verified the entire fixed combined candidate manifest and stored exact job IDs, fences, failed call IDs, input/output hashes, full original proof and affected dimensions in `dimension-cases-v663.json` (SHA-256 `d9faffa4cd3aa9ad0c1aba6c98a01d27963018d4d5567a1e0cab3b15558280aa`). It used metadata-only source checks; no PDF was opened, no model invoked and no canonical row changed.

Prepared `night_admit_dimensions_v663.py`, not scheduled or run against production. It requires the exact combined candidate `catalog-48cece41023e` to be active, verifies its full fixed manifest and exact pinned cases, enforces the night window before physical source verification and again before admission, checks full original proof and latest model event, and admits only the exact held job through the existing source-bound transactional recovery API. It saves independent per-case receipts and does not invoke a model itself. If a process exits between admission and receipt, inspect the durable owner request before any retry. # Progress: Implemented Time-of-Day Research Window (19:00 - 07:00) & Systemd Supervision

Added daily active research hours window support to Wisdom Question Orbit Runner (`orbit_runner.py`) per user requirement to avoid daytime execution and run exclusively during off-work hours (19:00 to 07:00 next morning).

1. Orbit Runner Enhancements (`src/uuma/orbit_runner.py`):
   - Added `parse_time_window(window_str)` supporting overnight wrap-around windows (e.g. '19:00-07:00') and daytime windows.
   - Added `is_time_in_window(current_time, start, end)` handling midnight boundary crossings.
   - Added `is_in_active_window(now)` on `OrbitRunner` respecting machine local timezone (`Asia/Kuching` UTC+8).
   - Added `--active-hours` CLI option and `UUMA_ORBIT_ACTIVE_HOURS` environment variable.
   - Guarded `run_once()`: when outside configured window, logs once and enters non-blocking sleep without claiming cycles or making external API calls.
   - Guarded idle timeout: idle timeout does not terminate runner when outside active window.
2. Unit Testing & Code Quality:
   - Added `test_parse_time_window`, `test_is_time_in_window`, and `test_orbit_runner_active_window_guards` in `tests/test_orbit_runtime.py`.
   - All tests passed (`pytest tests/test_orbit_runtime.py`).
   - Linted and formatted with Ruff (`ruff check src/uuma/orbit_runner.py tests/test_orbit_runtime.py`).
3. Production Deployment & Systemd Supervision (`alandelone-server-001`):
   - Synced updated `orbit_runner.py` to `/home/alandelone/.local/share/uuma/components/wisdom-daily-quota/ab2861c799c316346bb22d5de61f7eb377cbe19950d7bf584c3458cdd64e12e9/src/uuma/orbit_runner.py`.
   - Configured `UUMA_ORBIT_ACTIVE_HOURS=19:00-07:00` in `/home/alandelone/.local/share/uuma/wisdom-forge-cutover-20261022/wisdom-production.env`.
   - Upgraded systemd user service `uuma-wisdom-orbit.service` to `Restart=always`.
   - Started and verified under systemd (PID 917204): confirmed log output `Active research window configured: 19:00-07:00` and `Current time is outside active research window (19:00-07:00); sleeping owner=alandelone-server-001:917204`.

   - Fixed by adding `error_detail: str | None = None` and `__post_init__` to `RouteAttempt`.
   - Added regression unit tests to `tests/test_orbit_planner.py`.
2. Root Cause 2: `ValueError: The synthesis model did not return source-linked JSON after 3 attempts: malformed JSON.` in `src/uuma/orbit_synthesis.py`.
   - `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` produced markdown code blocks and unescaped quotes inside Chinese research text, causing strict `json.loads` failure across all 3 attempts.
   - Implemented `_parse_synthesis_points`: markdown fence stripping, trailing comma sanitization, control char cleanup, and regex-based key-value extraction fallback.
   - Added `UUMA_KAG_LLM_FALLBACK_MODEL` escalation on the 3rd attempt.
   - Added regression unit tests in `tests/test_orbit_runtime.py` covering markdown code fences, malformed JSON recovery, and model escalation.
   - Upgraded production model config in `wisdom-production.env` to `UUMA_KAG_LLM_MODEL=gemini-3.8-flash`.
3. Verification & Live Deployment:
   - Passed `tests/test_orbit_runtime.py -k test_compact_synthesis` (5/5 passed) and `ruff check`.
   - Created online SQLite backup `wisdom.db.pre-synthesis-20261103.bak` on `alandelone-server-001`.
   - Deployed updated `orbit_synthesis.py` to production component directory.
   - Transitioned `orbit_9ac4d099873b82f8f5a730cf07792419` to `QUEUED` and restarted runner daemon (PID 903741).

Returned coverage has 24 original IDs in order with missing positions 9, 10, 14, 15; all eight cited evidence IDs were supplied. Do not infer that the incomplete summary covers the missing observations.

Added source-only `RSTV4/src/rstv4/initial_digest_coverage_recovery.py` and a distinct `initial_digest_coverage_recovery` thinking prompt contract in `model_gateway.py`. The bounded helper accepts a source-pinned failed group/receipt from a future authorized caller, partitions groups to at most 12, assigns call-local compact identities, and requires actual task-specific replacement receipts, exact full ordered coverage per group, immediate-input evidence, context budget, and bounded nonempty summaries. # Session Handoff



## Current position



# Progress v625: retained artifact reread



The local, unintegrated RSTV4 retained-parse locator now records each verified artifact's size and SHA-256 and provides a reader that rechecks the bytes when consumed. This catches a change between locating a retained artifact and reading it. It does not deploy or connect the locator to the reading worker or archive.



Focused local tests: `test_retained_parse_locator_v623.py`: 7 passed, including changed-after-location and unknown-field rejection. Ruff on `src/rstv4/retained_parse_locator.py`: passed. Tests used local copied or synthetic artifacts; no NAS access and no model calls.



The earlier v622 local demonstration passes the audit file's own current digest as the expected value and does not establish independent provenance. Future controller/archive integration must obtain the pinned audit digest from an independently verified receipt. Neither the local locator nor the test asserts scientific acceptance or full-paper completion.



The user's choice about automatic night reading while NAS rescue is underway is still pending; existing policy has not been changed. The already authorized one extra retry concerned v329 and was consumed; the separate v419 hold has no approval for another model call. Preserve all existing results, the NAS rescue, the separate thread's work, and existing scheduled jobs.



Next: verify whether the NAS rescue has completed without reading the NAS; respect the pending scheduling choice if it remains active. Continue independent work when possible, then integrate only against a verifiable controller/archive receipt and validate a completed end-to-end reading when authorized and available.

# Progress note v621



- Revalidated 86 automatic READY initial jobs with zero attempts and no model events, plus two READY jobs with prior model history. Implemented `initial_synthesis_adoption.py`: append-only controller grant ties the exact frozen input digest, paper, parse, protocol and admission fence to one request and event; it does not alter the original job, priority, lease or input. Admission checks exact READY/fence/zero-attempt/no-model-history, active intent, verified parsed-source metadata, original PDF digest and quarantine. The normal worker still verifies the physical full PDF before reading. Existing historical jobs continue their frozen legacy protocol; unknown/conflicting/changed adoption history is rejected.

- Worker obtains the effective protocol under its current lease before initial processing and again in its guarded model path. `tests/test_initial_synthesis_adoption.py` verifies idempotency, unchanged job rows, actual compact worker path, daytime wait, and rejection for changed fence, started work, model history, manual priority, paused intent, source identity and missing/tampered adoption records. Relevant tests: 47 passed and Ruff passed after preserving initial import-order/unused-import lint failure; candidate release and production interpreter separately passed 10 focused worker/adoption tests.

- `compact-adoption-rehearsal-v618/receipt.json` documents a consistent owner-copy rehearsal for all 86: original job rows, source records, model events and earlier history preserved; all grants idempotent; zero real-owner writes and no model requests. `compact-adoption-reading-release-v617` fixed candidate `catalog-ee40f7e22480` passed read-only schema21 runtime admission. `compact-adoption-reading-activation-v619/receipt.json` records atomic idle-boundary activation of Reading only, Web unchanged, no activation DB writes/model submissions, and backup retained.

- `compact-adoption-live-v620/receipt.json` and `admitted.jsonl` record all 86 individually admitted at 2026-11-02 15:49 Malaysia, with zero job rows mutated and zero model calls. Every job was compared byte-for-byte to its rehearsed snapshot before and after; normal grant logic checked each exact source/fence and no history. Live read-only effective-protocol readback confirmed 86 READY compact and 2 READY legacy, with 86 grants. Do not insert duplicate grants, call models manually, or mark reading complete; automatic processing remains 00:00–08:00 Malaysia.

- The original two jobs with saved model calls remain on their prior protocol, already queued; observe them and the new compact jobs after nighttime execution. Their successful real model responses and semantic outcomes remain unverified. Deep v419 requires separate direct approval. Whole-goal actual FULL PaperSkeleton, portable archive/source adoption and light semantic review remain incomplete. Goal active, mission verification/pending_review; preserve unrelated workspace and all handoff history.

## Current position



Wisdom-Oldman Question Orbit AttributeError & FAILED Status Resolution (2026-11-02):

- Root cause: In `src/uuma/orbit_planner.py:246`, checking `traces[-1].error_detail` on a `RouteAttempt` during pending request polling threw `AttributeError: 'RouteAttempt' object has no attribute 'error_detail'`, as `RouteAttempt` lacked the `error_detail` field.

- Local fix: Extended `RouteAttempt` dataclass in `src/uuma/orbit_planner.py` with `error_detail: str | None = None` and `__post_init__` fallback to `self.reason` when `outcome != DiscoveryOutcome.SUCCESS`. Preserved `error_detail` across `to_discovery_attempt`, `to_dict`, and `traces.append` calls.

- Unit tests & lint: Added regression test `test_planner_pending_transient_failure_preserves_request_without_attribute_error` in `tests/test_orbit_planner.py`. All 15 tests in `test_orbit_planner.py` passed; Ruff checks passed with zero errors.

- Server fix & publication: Created an online SQLite consistency backup of production `wisdom.db` on `ubuntu-server` (size 1.16GB: `wisdom.db.pre-resolve-20261102.bak`). Backed up and synced patched `orbit_planner.py` to `~/.local/share/uuma/components/wisdom-daily-quota/ab2861c799c316346bb22d5de61f7eb377cbe19950d7bf584c3458cdd64e12e9/src/uuma/orbit_planner.py`. Verified syntax compilation. Stopped idling `orbit_runner` PID 698780.

- Orbit resumption: Transitioned `orbit_9ac4d099873b82f8f5a730cf07792419` from `FAILED` (`stop_reason = "'RouteAttempt' object has no attribute 'error_detail'"`) to `QUEUED` (`stop_reason = None`) via `QuestionOrbitService.transition`.

- Runner restart & live verification: Restarted `orbit_runner` on `ubuntu-server` (PID 874803). The runner claimed `orbit_9ac4d099873b82f8f5a730cf07792419` on cycle `cycle_47219eb08e9d4515b616868e04af6527` (attempt 1) and is actively running. Observatory probe verified live: `orbit_info.status = ACTIVE`, `runner_live = True`, `lease_owner = alandelone-server-001:874803`. Frontend display `https://agts.95963210.xyz/?item=wisdom_topic_topic_07a8574b5d504d34a5483407c2a88400` displays `🟢 现场调研中 (ACTIVE)`.

## Current position



# Progress note v616



- Implemented separately versioned compact initial synthesis tasks and protocol in RSTV4 source. Each digest/synthesis/theme call maps original input and evidence IDs to local aliases, then restores only exact aliases. The request includes SHA-256 of the original mapping to prevent durable operation collisions between identical aliases bound to different evidence. Original raw model event, task and prompt version remain actual; computed projection is retained in the derived synthesis contract. No fuzzy selector repair or source validation weakening. Compact reduction fan-in is limited to 12 to address the observed incomplete coverage; original input coverage and immediate-source evidence assertions remain strict.

- New initial jobs carry `initial-whole-paper-v3-compact-refs` in their frozen input. Existing IDs are unchanged; existing inputs without that field remain legacy. Worker refuses cross-protocol tasks. First integration returned 48 pass/4 fail due legacy fixtures assuming old task names. Preserved full old test source `initial-synthesis-test-before-compact-context-v611.py` and recorded explicit legacy fixture context in `rules/testing-contracts.md`; no prior assertions were weakened. Separate compact worker tests verify actual pause/resume receipt reuse and reject unknown citations without publication. 54 relevant tests passed; after adding mapping-binding and bounded fan-in, 30 focused compact tests passed again, as did Ruff. The fixed release and production interpreter passed the same 30 compact checks.

- `compact-synthesis-reading-release-v612` prepared fixed Reading candidate `catalog-ad6ed9be956e`, resolver read-only admitted schema21, and `compact-synthesis-reading-activation-v613/receipt.json` records owned Reading-only atomic idle activation, existing Web unchanged, no explicit model calls or database writes by activation, backup retained. `compact-synthesis-web-release-v614` used the separate current Web baseline, adding only the four relevant sources and preserving partial-candidate UI. Resolver and pinned Web HTTP fixture checks passed; `compact-synthesis-web-activation-v615/receipt.json` records Web activation and live partial-candidate readback, with Reading worker identities and pointer unchanged. `test_initial_compact_web.py` passed on the fixed Web engine and confirms a new HTTP-requested job pins compact protocol.

- Current canonical snapshot after activation: 20 NEEDS_HUMAN, 88 READY, 1 SUCCEEDED; all existing 109 jobs are legacy. Of READY jobs, 86 have attempts=0 and no model events, and two are the already admitted prior recoveries with historical calls. Their statuses remained READY. New protocol currently applies to newly created jobs only; no actual compact-model inference or full scientific result has been observed. Never represent the 86 pre-existing jobs as migrated or fixed.

- Next design a scoped append-only adoption mechanism for existing zero-attempt/no-model-event jobs, preserving original frozen request bytes and active/manual/night policy, with a versioned durable authorization/event and source/fence/idempotency checks. Do not migrate the two jobs with prior model events or force night work during day. Verify on a disposable owner before use. Observe the two already admitted jobs during their next scheduled night. Deep v419 requires separate direct approval; original FULL sample, archive/source portability/adoption and light semantic review remain incomplete. Mission verification/pending_review and Goal active.

- New initial jobs carry `initial-whole-paper-v3-compact-refs` in their frozen input. Existing IDs are unchanged; existing inputs without that field remain legacy. Worker refuses cross-protocol tasks. First integration returned 48 pass/4 fail due legacy fixtures assuming old task names. Preserved full old test source `initial-synthesis-test-before-compact-context-v611.py` and recorded explicit legacy fixture context in `rules/testing-contracts.md`; no prior assertions were weakened. Separate compact worker tests verify actual pause/resume receipt reuse and reject unknown citations without publication. 54 relevant tests passed; after adding mapping-binding and bounded fan-in, 30 focused compact tests passed again, as did Ruff. The fixed release and production interpreter passed the same 30 compact checks.

- `compact-synthesis-reading-release-v612` prepared fixed Reading candidate `catalog-ad6ed9be956e`, resolver read-only admitted schema21, and `compact-synthesis-reading-activation-v613/receipt.json` records owned Reading-only atomic idle activation, existing Web unchanged, no explicit model calls or database writes by activation, backup retained. `compact-synthesis-web-release-v614` used the separate current Web baseline, adding only the four relevant sources and preserving partial-candidate UI. Resolver and pinned Web HTTP fixture checks passed; `compact-synthesis-web-activation-v615/receipt.json` records Web activation and live partial-candidate readback, with Reading worker identities and pointer unchanged. `test_initial_compact_web.py` passed on the fixed Web engine and confirms a new HTTP-requested job pins compact protocol.

- Current canonical snapshot after activation: 20 NEEDS_HUMAN, 88 READY, 1 SUCCEEDED; all existing 109 jobs are legacy. Of READY jobs, 86 have attempts=0 and no model events, and two are the already admitted prior recoveries with historical calls. Their statuses remained READY. New protocol currently applies to newly created jobs only; no actual compact-model inference or full scientific result has been observed. Never represent the 86 pre-existing jobs as migrated or fixed.

- Next design a scoped append-only adoption mechanism for existing zero-attempt/no-model-event jobs, preserving original frozen request bytes and active/manual/night policy, with a versioned durable authorization/event and source/fence/idempotency checks. Do not migrate the two jobs with prior model events or force night work during day. Verify on a disposable owner before use. Observe the two already admitted jobs during their next scheduled night. Deep v419 requires separate direct approval; original FULL sample, archive/source portability/adoption and light semantic review remain incomplete. Mission verification/pending_review and Goal active.

- Started a scalable fix in product source `src/rstv4/initial_compact_synthesis_refs.py`, currently NOT wired into synthesis, jobs, prompts or running releases. Actual original IDs are retained; it does not use fuzzy matching or invent citations. 16 focused tests in `tests/test_initial_compact_synthesis_refs.py` passed; Ruff passed after retaining the first import-order error.

- Next integrate as a new separately versioned whole-paper synthesis protocol with compact aliases for digest, final synthesis and theme-role phases. Then prepare safe candidate and release only after checks. Do not claim this utility has fixed live jobs yet.

- Preserve distinct pending deep v419 approval, all existing state, separate night queue and ongoing user work. Actual FULL PaperSkeleton, portable source/archive/adoption and light semantic review remain incomplete. Goal active; mission verification/pending_review.
